  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B001: Measured amount makes his claimed expenditure assessable rather than rhetorically boundless.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B001: An uttered statement turns inward supposition into an externally inspectable boast.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B009: Exhaustion and depletion give the boast the form of claimed resource outflow.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Acquiring and multiplying wealth supplies the stock from which the speaker claims to spend.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: A mass piled layer upon layer makes abundance materially countable despite its boastful scale.

## visibility enforcement
- reading: He believes an unseen act is unenforceable, so lack of a witness is converted into lack of anyone able to call him to account.
- mechanism: The repeated frame substitutes seeing for having power. This creates an abductive chain in which presumed invisibility licenses presumed impunity: if no witness saw the act, he reasons, no agent can effectively act upon him.
- trace:
  - 90:5 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B006: Practical accountability supplies the enforcement function whose informational basis is tested by the parallel verse.
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B003: Capacity reaching effective control is re-read as enforceability rather than raw strength.
  - 90:5 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B002: Exhaustive anyone establishes the universal absence of an enforcer that the speaker imagines.
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B002: The repeated supposition marks unseen action and untouchability as two linked beliefs.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight supplies the missing observation on which his theory of non-enforcement depends.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B002: The same exhaustive anyone seals the syntactic parallel between absent controller and absent witness.

## endowed evidence
- reading: His very capacity and the channels that can disclose its use are endowed, embodied, and oriented toward visible alternatives.
- mechanism: Made eyes, communicative organs, and manifest routes turn the subject's own body and orientation into evidence infrastructure. His capacity is granted, and the means of seeing, reporting, and choosing accompany him.
- trace:
  - 90:5 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B010: Seeking and testing information makes the focus supposition vulnerable to embodied evidence.
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B003: Effective capacity is revised from autonomous possession to an ability whose instruments were supplied.
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B001: Making and producing supplies the external origin of the faculties on which his agency depends.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B003: The eye of care and supervision makes sight an organ of watchfulness as well as reception.
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B008: Conveying a message makes the tongue a channel by which hidden conduct can become report.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B002: Speech passing from lip to lip supplies interpersonal transmission rather than sealed privacy.
  - 90:10 **وَهَدَيْنَٰهُ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path supplies disclosed orientation and accountable choice.
  - 90:10 **ٱلنَّجْدَيْنِ** ن ج د B002: Manifest appearance makes the two routes legible rather than hidden.

## capacity reversal
- reading: Power is tested by whether he can cross resistance and use capacity to remove power from over someone else.
- mechanism: The question of who can act upon him is reversed into what he can bring himself to do. He projects power outward, yet the sequence tests capacity by a hard ascent and specifies its use as releasing another person from enclosure.
- trace:
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B003: Effective capacity is the focus hinge that can reverse from domination suffered to agency exercised.
  - 90:11 **ٱقْتَحَمَ** ق ح م B002: Perilous difficulties of a route materialize the demanding action his claimed power does not perform.
  - 90:11 **ٱلْعَقَبَةَ** ع ق ب B012: The difficult rising obstacle gives capacity a resistant object rather than a victim.
  - 90:12 **أَدْرَىٰكَ** د ر ي B001: Knowledge and discernment interrupt the speaker's self-sufficient estimate by requiring disclosed understanding.
  - 90:13 **فَكُّ** ف ك ك B002: Releasing a pledge or neck from enclosure redirects effective power toward liberation.
  - 90:13 **رَقَبَةٍ** ر ق ب B004: The neck makes the object of release a concrete embodied person under another's power.

## vulnerable claimants
- reading: He supposes that not even one vulnerable person can have a binding claim upon him or reduce his hoarded surplus.
- mechanism: Feeding amid famine identifies isolated and abased persons whose need can bear upon accumulated wealth. The exhaustive 'anyone' becomes concrete: each vulnerable person can constitute a moral claim عَلَيْهِ, and meeting that claim narrows his surplus.
- trace:
  - 90:5 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B006: Practical accountability lets another person's need become an inspectable claim on conduct.
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B004: Narrowing provision gives the claimant's demand a real cost to the speaker's abundance.
  - 90:5 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B005: Isolation individual by individual prepares the shift from abstract nobody to each socially isolated person.
  - 90:14 **إِطْعَٰمٌ** ط ع م B002: Feeding another turns wealth into an outward transfer answering embodied need.
  - 90:14 **يَوْمٍ** ي و م B003: A day's severe event makes the obligation situationally urgent rather than abstract.
  - 90:14 **مَسْغَبَةٍ** س غ ب B001: Hunger with exhaustion supplies the bodily pressure exerted by unmet need.
  - 90:15 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: A child cut off from a maintainer identifies a claimant whose dependence lacks its expected support.
  - 90:15 **مَقْرَبَةٍ** ق ر ب B003: Kinship makes the supposedly powerless individual's claim socially near and relationally specific.
  - 90:16 **مِسْكِينًا** س ك ن B006: The abasement of destitution supplies a person stripped of ordinary leverage yet still morally pressing.
  - 90:16 **مَتْرَبَةٍ** ت ر ب B002: Poverty as adhesion to dust locates the claimant at the material floor of the speaker's piled wealth.

## reciprocal answerability
- reading: Rightly formed agency includes chosen reciprocal answerability, where others counsel him and he bears responsibility for others.
- mechanism: Becoming part of a trusting group that exchanges counsel, bears responsibility, and practices mercy replaces the isolated sovereign self with reciprocal formation. Other people legitimately act upon him and are sustained by him.
- trace:
  - 90:5 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B005: Isolation and individual distribution expose the atomized social picture inside his claim of nobody over him.
  - 90:5 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B006: Accountable management gives reciprocal counsel a focus-anchored alternative to unanswerable autonomy.
  - 90:17 **كَانَ** ك و ن B003: Sponsorship and standing over another supplies responsibility as a mode of belonging.
  - 90:17 **ءَامَنُوا۟** ء م ن B001: Settled trust supplies the relational security in which mutual influence need not be domination.
  - 90:17 **وَتَوَاصَوْا۟ وَتَوَاصَوْا۟** و ص ي B003: Exchanged counsel among a group directly supplies reciprocal action person upon person.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلصَّبْرِ** ص ب ر B003: Bearing responsibility and remaining attached give mutual counsel durable social force.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلْمَرْحَمَةِ** ر ح م B001: Tender mercy directs reciprocal power toward care rather than mastery.

## reassigned standing
- reading: His standing is not self-certified: response, affiliation, and orientation place him within a class that can be named and judged.
- mechanism: The social merit he may count for himself is displaced by mirrored companionship and right-left classification. Standing is not a private hoard that blocks every person; it emerges from response to visible signs and durable affiliation.
- trace:
  - 90:5 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B004: Counted merits and social honor supply the focus-side status that the closing classification reassigns.
  - 90:5 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B005: Isolation by individuals contrasts with the later identity of companions belonging to a side.
  - 90:18 **أَصْحَٰبُ** ص ح ب B001: Companionship and lasting association make standing a shared affiliation rather than solitary prestige.
  - 90:18 **ٱلْمَيْمَنَةِ** ي م ن B004: Right as power and rightful claim reassigns effective standing to an oriented class.
  - 90:19 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B003: Concealing truth explains how visible grounds of accountability can be actively screened off.
  - 90:19 **بِـَٔايَٰتِنَا** ء ي ي B003: A manifest sign supplies the disclosed criterion that private self-reckoning refuses.
  - 90:19 **أَصْحَٰبُ** ص ح ب B001: The repeated companionship branch creates a formally matched but opposed collective identity.
  - 90:19 **ٱلْمَشْـَٔمَةِ** ش ء م B001: The left side supplies the opposed orientation by which the two companion groups are sorted.

## sealed over
- reading: The relation he declares impossible returns at the close as a sealed condition over the class to which such self-reckoning leads.
- mechanism: The final image spatially reverses the denied relation. The man supposes no one can impose constriction عَلَيْهِ; the close presents fire and an emphatically shut enclosure عَلَيْهِمْ over a plural class.
- trace:
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B004: Constriction to a narrow measure supplies the focus-side condition that he declares impossible.
  - 90:5 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B005: Individual isolation is reversed when the supposedly untouchable singular is read toward an enclosed plural class.
  - 90:20 **نَارٌ** ن و ر B002: Kindled fire supplies the enclosing medium that is now actually over them.
  - 90:20 **مُّؤْصَدَةٌۢ** و ص د B001: Closing and clamping over something makes the denied constriction spatially explicit.
  - 90:20 **مُّؤْصَدَةٌۢ** و ص د B001: The split root's second closure inventory reinforces a door sealed with deliberate firmness.

## wealth cushion
- reading: His piled wealth acts in his imagination like cushioning that props him up and absorbs every attempt to press power upon him.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:5 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B008: A cushion or prop supplies the material image of a self protected from pressure.
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B003: Effective control supplies the pressure that his imagined padding is meant to absorb.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Accumulated wealth supplies the material from which social insulation is built.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B001: Matter felted layer over layer turns the wealth heap into figurative padding around the self.

## culinary containment
- reading: As a contained material resonance, he imagines himself and his abundance beyond anyone's power to place within measure, transform, and portion into nourishment.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B007: The cooking pot supplies a bounded vessel in which abundance is subjected to measure and transformation.
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B007: The cloth used to lower a pot unexpectedly supplies the handling mechanism for the focus vessel image.
  - 90:14 **إِطْعَٰمٌ** ط ع م B002: Feeding another gives the contained yield a social destination rather than leaving it hoarded.
  - 90:14 **مَسْغَبَةٍ** س غ ب B001: Famine and exhausting hunger make portioning stored resources materially urgent.

## reported witness
- reading: Even absent an eyewitness, inquiry and circulating report can make the act discoverable and therefore answerable.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:5 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B010: Seeking out and testing information supplies investigation as a route past presumed secrecy.
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B003: Effective control becomes reachable once information about the act can circulate.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B007: Speech spreading among people supplies a public channel that the boaster cannot fully contain.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B003: The non-dominant mapped branch of narrating reports turns sight into a secondary trace of tellability.


# Focus 90:6

## destroyed heap
- reading: He openly claims to have made a piled concentration of owned resources perish; destructive agency and heap structure remain audible.
- mechanism: The speaker presents himself as the agent who made a concentrated stock of property perish. The heap image makes quantity spatial and material, while the destruction verb is stronger than neutral transfer or expenditure.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B001: Uttered speech makes the line an overt self-report whose wording is itself part of the event.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B001: Ruin and extinction give the causative verb the force of making resources disappear, not merely moving them.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Acquired property supplies the stock over which the speaker claims destructive agency.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: Gathered abundance piled upon itself turns the amount into a visible concentrated heap.

## self authorizing report
- reading: The sentence is also a public performance: the speaker offers the scale of an alleged loss as its own proof and verdict.
- mechanism: The utterance can operate as circulating self-display and as an attempted verdict on the speaker's own standing. A false or inflated report remains live without being presumed: saying that a heap was destroyed tries to make the asserted cost authorize the speaker.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B007: Circulating public talk lets the first-person statement function as reputation-making rather than private bookkeeping.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B010: Arrogating judgment supplies the social move by which the speaker tries to pronounce his own expenditure sufficient.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B005: False saying keeps exaggeration or misattribution available as a risk of the boast, not as a settled accusation.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B001: Claimed ruin provides the costly deed that the utterance converts into social credit.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: The heaped amount supplies impressive scale for the attempted self-authorization.

## exhaustion and futility
- reading: He may also be pleading that a whole reserve was exhausted in strenuous, perhaps futile, effort.
- mechanism: The claim can be heard as a self-assessment of resources worn out through strenuous, possibly pointless effort. This reading does not erase the boast; it preserves a coexisting plea that the speaker has been depleted by what he undertook.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B011: Saying with the force of supposing lets the utterance carry the speaker's own assessment of what his effort amounted to.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B009: Exhausting effort shifts destruction toward resources consumed by toil.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B011: The valley of futility keeps open the possibility that the costly effort produced no worthy result.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Wealth supplies the finite reserve depleted by the exertion.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: The accumulated heap magnifies both the claimed effort and the possible waste.

## material decompaction
- reading: A dense, immobilized stock was broken down; whether that is waste, release, or conversion remains open.
- mechanism: Quantity becomes material topology: wealth is not only numerous but felted, layered, and immobilized. Destroying it can therefore be imagined as breaking down a compact stock, with the ethical value of that decompaction still wholly undecided at baseline.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Possessed wealth provides the material stock being compacted.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B001: Layer-over-layer compaction gives the wealth pile a dense material structure.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B003: Sticking in place adds immobilization to the image of accumulated resources.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B001: Destruction supplies the force that dismantles or eliminates the compact stock.

## hardship plea
- reading: I exhausted a whole reserve under severe effort, a plea that can coexist with the boast.
- mechanism: Human hardship activates the exhausting-effort branch of the focus verb. The same sentence can therefore sound less like carefree largesse and more like a speaker pleading that accumulated resources were consumed under strain.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B001: The overt report is the form in which the speaker voices his account of depletion.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B009: Exhausting effort allows the claimed destruction to be heard as depletion through toil.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: The heap marks the scale of the reserve allegedly exhausted.
  - 90:4 **كَبَدٍ** ك ب د B002: Hardship supplies the surrounding condition that can turn a boast of loss into a plea of burden.

## external accounting
- reading: The expenditure is an unverified self-entry in a larger reckoning; neither its amount nor its meaning is self-certified.
- mechanism: Oath, counting, measure, and contested capacity place the self-report inside an external audit. The speaker's quantity claim no longer proves either the event or his power; it becomes an entry to be measured against a standard he does not control.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B001: Uttered speech identifies the claim that enters the audit.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B005: The false-saying branch keeps accuracy open as a question rather than allowing the amount to authenticate itself.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: The heaped amount is the magnitude the speaker submits as evidence.
  - 90:1 **أُقْسِمُ** ق س م B004: An oath distributed to its parties supplies a higher frame of attestation around the man's mere assertion.
  - 90:5 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting turn the vague boast of abundance into something answerable to reckoning.
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B001: A measure reaching its limit supplies the external scale against which the claimed heap is tested.
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B003: Effective power separates possessing means from actually controlling the judgment or outcome.

## life conversion test
- reading: The claim is ethically underdescribed: loss becomes meaningful only if the heap is converted into generated or sustained life.
- mechanism: Generation, creation, feeding another, and a child cut off from a caregiver establish a life-producing counterflow to annihilation. The missing beneficiary of the focus claim becomes diagnostic: costly loss is not yet life-giving expenditure.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B001: Extinction supplies the focus action whose outcome is tested against generation and sustenance.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Owned resources provide the convertible input.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: Concentrated abundance makes the absence of any named recipient especially conspicuous.
  - 90:3 **وَوَالِدٍ وَلَدَ** و ل د B005: Something generated from something else supplies a productive transformation opposed to bare disappearance.
  - 90:4 **خَلَقْنَا** خ ل ق B002: Bringing creation into being sharpens the contrast between generative use and annihilated stock.
  - 90:14 **إِطْعَٰمٌ** ط ع م B002: Feeding another gives wealth a concrete path into sustaining a body.
  - 90:15 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: A child cut off from a caregiver supplies the vulnerable life toward which converted wealth could be directed.

## embodied witness
- reading: His claim is an embodied testimony issued through visible gifts and already open to observation, circulation, and contradiction.
- mechanism: The claim is produced by a tongue between lips and exposed to sight and watchfulness. Speech is therefore neither disembodied nor private: the very capacities that enable the boast also make it observable and answerable.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B001: Articulated speech is the bodily act placed under observation.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B005: Possible false saying states what embodied witnessing can expose without predetermining guilt.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Vision by eye or insight supplies the witnessing that contests presumed invisibility.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B003: The eye of watchful care adds sustained oversight rather than a momentary glance.
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B001: The tongue supplies the instrument that turns an inner claim into accountable sound.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B002: Speech passing from lip to lip makes circulation and reception part of the claim's social life.

## cost is not ascent
- reading: The fortune may have been exhausted in motion that never crossed the decisive rise; cost is not equivalent to ascent.
- mechanism: Direction, elevation, reckless entry into hardship, and the difficult rise separate expense from achievement. A heap can be exhausted through undirected plunging; only a directed crossing makes the cost part of ascent.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B009: Exhausting effort supplies costly motion that may or may not reach a worthy destination.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: The immense heap measures cost but does not by itself prove passage.
  - 90:10 **وَهَدَيْنَٰهُ** ه د ي B001: Gentle direction toward a road or truth supplies the orientation missing from bare expenditure.
  - 90:10 **ٱلنَّجْدَيْنِ** ن ج د B001: Elevation turns the path into an ascent with a real change of level.
  - 90:11 **ٱقْتَحَمَ** ق ح م B001: Plunging into hardship without deliberation supplies a costly but potentially misdirected alternative.
  - 90:11 **ٱلْعَقَبَةَ** ع ق ب B012: The difficult rising obstacle supplies the threshold that expenditure alone does not cross.

## unlocking compacted capital
- reading: The heap can be worth destroying when its compaction is liquidated into the unfastening of a bound life.
- mechanism: Opening what is locked and releasing a pledged neck provide a constructive direction for destroying a heap. The focus can now be read as liquidation or decompaction: accumulated property is broken apart so that a bound life is unfastened.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B001: Causative destruction supplies the dismantling force, whose value now depends on what it releases.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Possessed wealth is the capital stock available for liquidation.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B001: Layered compaction gives the stock the structure that must be broken open.
  - 90:13 **فَكُّ** ف ك ك B001: Opening a closure and separating an entanglement converts destruction into purposeful decompaction.
  - 90:13 **فَكُّ** ف ك ك B002: Releasing collateral or a neck gives the opened wealth a concrete liberating outcome.
  - 90:13 **رَقَبَةٍ** ر ق ب B012: Capital or the pure core of property forms a lexical bridge between the wealth heap and what can purchase release.
  - 90:13 **رَقَبَةٍ** ر ق ب B004: The neck supplies the bound person whose freedom distinguishes release from waste.

## abundance reassembled as relation
- reading: A pile of things is judged by whether its breakup produces a joined company of people rather than a larger solitary reputation.
- mechanism: The context opposes two forms of gathering: things piled together and people joined through kinship, reciprocal counsel, mercy, and companionship. Wealth ceases to be a socially inert mass when its decompaction repairs isolation and builds relation.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: Piled abundance supplies a dense gathering of things that can be contrasted with a gathering of persons.
  - 90:15 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B002: Isolation and loss of a peer expose the social deficit that a mere property heap cannot answer.
  - 90:15 **مَقْرَبَةٍ** ق ر ب B003: Kinship and lineage direct redistributed wealth toward an already binding relation.
  - 90:17 **وَتَوَاصَوْا۟ وَتَوَاصَوْا۟** و ص ي B001: Joining one thing to another supplies the connective mechanics of community.
  - 90:17 **وَتَوَاصَوْا۟ وَتَوَاصَوْا۟** و ص ي B003: Reciprocal counsel makes relation mutual rather than a donor's solitary performance.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلْمَرْحَمَةِ** ر ح م B002: Womb-kinship joins mercy to a generative bond instead of an abstract sentiment.
  - 90:18 **أَصْحَٰبُ** ص ح ب B001: Companionship supplies the resulting social assembly that can replace an assembly of wealth.

## stuck heap and stuck person
- reading: Worthy destruction unsticks concentrated resources and a person stuck in destitution by setting support in motion between them.
- mechanism: The wealth pile and the destitute person share immobilization: one sticks in accumulated place, the other is stilled and pressed to dust. Purposeful expenditure becomes a transfer that unsticks both sides rather than a theatrical disappearance.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Owned wealth supplies the immobilized resource side of the relation.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B003: Staying put and sticking to the ground make the accumulated stock materially immobile.
  - 90:16 **مِسْكِينًا** س ك ن B001: Loss of movement supplies the human immobility mirrored by stuck wealth.
  - 90:16 **مِسْكِينًا** س ك ن B006: Humbling destitution gives that immobility its social force.
  - 90:16 **مَتْرَبَةٍ** ت ر ب B002: Poverty figured as sticking to dust completes the spatial analogy with the grounded heap.

## material trace speaks
- reading: The wealth's material and social aftermath becomes a rival utterance: its traces say whether the heap became release, nourishment, waste, or concealment.
- mechanism: A thing can 'say' by indicating, while a sign and a visible beacon make consequences legible. The focus now contains rival speakers: the man's mouth reports destruction, but the remaining material and social traces can state what the wealth actually became.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B014: A thing's saying as indication lets outcomes speak alongside the man's verbal report.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B001: Claimed disappearance creates the evidentiary question of what effects, residues, or beneficiaries remain.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B001: Layered material gives the alleged expenditure a substrate capable of leaving observable traces.
  - 90:19 **بِـَٔايَٰتِنَا** ء ي ي B003: A manifest sign supplies nonverbal evidence against which the self-report can be read.
  - 90:20 **نَارٌ** ن و ر B005: A visible beacon supplies the image of consequences made conspicuous rather than hidden in speech.

## cover story and enclosure
- reading: The declaration of disappearance may cover an immobilized conversion, or name a real burn whose sealed outcome is the reverse of liberating outflow.
- mechanism: Covering and doubled images of tight closure reactivate false saying and stuck compaction. The claim that wealth is gone may conceal retained value, status consumption, or a sealed destructive outcome; alternatively, the wealth truly became fuel but produced enclosure rather than release.
- trace:
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B005: False saying supplies the possible cover-story mechanism without deciding that the speaker lied.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B003: Sticking in place keeps open the possibility that value remained immobilized despite the claim of destruction.
  - 90:19 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B001: Covering supplies the act by which a declaration of loss could hide what the expenditure actually produced.
  - 90:20 **نَارٌ** ن و ر B002: Kindled fire supplies the alternative in which wealth really is consumed but only as destructive fuel.
  - 90:20 **مُّؤْصَدَةٌۢ** و ص د B001: Closing tightly over something supplies enclosure as the opposite outcome to opening and release.
  - 90:20 **مُّؤْصَدَةٌۢ** و ص د B001: The non-dominant mapped branch independently reinforces a door sealed with deliberate firmness.

## abundance to fewness
- reading: The same sentence can faintly undercut him as an admission that he converted abundance into littleness or impoverishment.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B001: The non-dominant mapped branch of fewness supplies the endpoint latent inside the act of claiming depletion.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B001: Destruction supplies the transition that can reduce the stock.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Wealth supplies what moves from possession toward paucity.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: Heaped abundance supplies the starting pole of the reversal.

## wealth entanglement
- reading: Wealth becomes an entangling, compact structure: the claim may describe tearing free of it or perishing within it.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B002: The reported spider term supplies the remote image of an entangling structure.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B001: Layered compaction gives the speculative web a dense accumulated body.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B003: Sticking in place supplies the entanglement's immobilizing effect.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B001: Destruction can then be imagined as either tearing the web apart or being ruined inside it.

## heap degraded to waste
- reading: The heap can be abrasively revalued as compacted waste, making the boast an inadvertent confession of futile consumption.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B005: The clump of dung and urine degrades the prestigious heap into an accumulated residue.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B011: Futility turns costly consumption into a result without durable value.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Actual property supplies the high-value input whose residue is being satirically revalued.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B007: Public talk supplies the boast that the degrading material image reverses.

## ready cash and reward
- reading: A remote ledger reading hears a claim of burning through a piled fund of cash assigned as payments or rewards.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B001: Destruction supplies the depletion of the proposed liquid fund.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Wealth supplies the broad asset category narrowed by the contextual branches.
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B005: A reward assigned for work supplies a possible payout category for the alleged expenditure.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B011: Ready cash supplies liquidity rather than an undifferentiated property heap.


# Focus 90:7

## zero witness supposition
- reading: The question diagnoses self-exemption: his own uncertain estimate tries to erase every sensory and insightful witness.
- mechanism: A private supposition constructs a zero-witness condition. The exhaustive force of أَحَدٌ enlarges this from one missing eyewitness to an erased class of sensory or insightful observers, while the object pronoun in يَرَهُ makes the reckoner himself the exposed object.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B002: Supposition supplies a provisional inner estimate and functions as the mental act that posits an observerless condition.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight supplies the denied relation and makes both sensory notice and perceptive recognition live.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B002: Exhaustive anyone under negation expands the supposed absence across the whole class of possible witnesses.

## zero witness ledger
- reading: He has entered the witness count as zero; the question reopens that ledger and makes his reckoning itself subject to reckoning.
- mechanism: The ayah can be modeled as a defective ledger. The subject performs a reckoning, enters no single observer in the account, and is then made reckonable by the very question that exposes this omission.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting turn the subject's belief into a ledger-like computation rather than a free-floating feeling.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B003: One in counting supplies the missing unit whose absence lets the subject enter a witness total of zero.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Actual or insightful seeing is the event that would falsify the zero entered in that ledger.

## asymmetric visibility field
- reading: The subject imagines an asymmetric world in which he may assess and act while no facing other can return the gaze.
- mechanism: Mutual visibility is broken into an asymmetry: he remains an assessing subject but imagines himself unavailable as another's object. The isolation branch of أَحَدٌ makes this a self-made blind spot rather than simple physical darkness.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B002: Supposition gives the subject the unilateral mental position from which he designs the visibility field.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B004: Mutual visibility and facing supply the reciprocal field that his belief attempts to interrupt on only one side.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B005: Isolation individual by individual gives the imagined blind spot its social form: a self detached from every facing other.

## audience merit economy
- reading: The absence of all spectators becomes a test of audience-made merit: what remains of his self-credit when nobody can applaud the display?
- mechanism: Visibility may be the medium in which social credit is minted. By asking what happens when no one sees him, the ayah can strip away spectator-dependent worth and test whether the actor's self-valuation survives without an audience.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B004: Counted merits and social standing supply the credit the subject may believe his visible conduct has accumulated.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B005: Showing an act so people see it supplies the spectator economy whose removal tests the act's claimed value.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B002: Exhaustive no one removes every human spectator, not merely a preferred audience.

## bounded public witness field
- reading: He attempts to declare himself unseen while already located inside a sworn, bounded, inhabited field of human detectability.
- mechanism: The opening context builds a sworn, bounded, inhabited, sense-capable field before the focus question. Within that field, 'no one' is not neutral emptiness but an attempted withdrawal from a place where testimony, location, residence, and human perception already intersect.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B004: Mutual visibility supplies the focus relation that the civic field makes harder to sever.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B005: Individual isolation names the withdrawal that the surrounding network of place and people resists.
  - 90:1 **أُقْسِمُ** ق س م B004: An oath apportioned among its parties supplies a formal testimony frame and makes the later seeing question evidentiary.
  - 90:1 **ٱلْبَلَدِ** ب ل د B001: A bounded tract supplies a determinate scene in which the subject is locatable rather than placeless.
  - 90:2 **حِلٌّۢ** ح ل ل B002: Dwelling or alighting in a place turns the bounded scene into an inhabited witness field.
  - 90:4 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B002: Sensing a thing by sight, hearing, or perception supplies human detectability inside that field.

## power is not invisibility
- reading: It also breaks his equation of impunity with invisibility: a witness may be present even where sanction is not yet exercised.
- mechanism: The near-identical question in 90:5 changes only the decisive predicate: no one can exercise power over him becomes no one has seen him. The repetition exposes a possible illicit inference from lack of immediate restraint to lack of observation; latent evidence need not arrive as immediate coercion.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B002: Supposition is the repeated mental operator through which the subject conflates two different absences.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing supplies evidentiary presence even when no restraining force is presently visible.
  - 90:5 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B002: The same supposition branch fixes the mental frame across both questions so the predicate change becomes diagnostic.
  - 90:5 **يَقْدِرَ** ق د ر B003: Effective capacity, action, and control supply the coercive power that the first question denies.
  - 90:5 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B002: Exhaustive no one repeats the same universal scope on the power side of the comparison.

## boast as auditable expenditure
- reading: A specific self-crediting expenditure claim is placed under audit: who or what can verify the conversion of piled wealth into the event he reports?
- mechanism: The direct claim about exhausting piled wealth gives sight a forensic object. The issue becomes correspondence between utterance and event: was wealth actually depleted, how, and with what visible result? The focus question opens an audit without deciding in advance that the boast is false.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B001: Accounting turns the follow-up question into scrutiny of a quantitative expenditure claim.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing supplies the observation needed to compare the spoken account with the event.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B005: The branch possibility of saying or attributing what did not occur opens an evidentiary gap without proving that this particular claim is false.
  - 90:6 **أَهْلَكْتُ** ه ل ك B009: Exhausting effort or spending it out supplies depletion as the claimed event to be checked.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Possessing and accumulating wealth supplies the value-bearing stock in the speaker's account.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: A mass piled layer over layer makes the claimed quantity conspicuous and gives the boast measurable weight.

## endowed eyes reverse the gaze
- reading: The actor is also an endowed seer: denying the returned gaze exposes his refusal to inhabit a reciprocal and potentially caring field of vision.
- mechanism: The next question gives the supposedly unseen man two eyes. This reverses the visual vector: he is not only an object under a possible gaze but an endowed observer whose own capacity to notice is accountable. Watching can also shade into care, so visibility becomes a responsibility he possesses, not only a threat imposed on him.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B004: Mutual visibility supplies the reversible relation in which the seen person can also become a seeing counterpart.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B005: Isolation supplies the self-enclosed posture that the bodily endowment breaks open.
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B001: Making and bringing a thing about supplies the eyes as a deliberate endowment rather than an accidental capacity.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B001: The observing eye materializes in the subject the very faculty presupposed by the focus question.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B003: Watchful preservation and care let the endowed gaze carry an ethical function beyond bare optical intake.

## visible route makes choice legible
- reading: It also denies an ignorance alibi: he is equipped for insight and placed before manifest routes, so his refusal of the costly path becomes readable.
- mechanism: Guidance, exposed heights, a hazardous incursion, a difficult rise, and knowledge turn seeing into practical legibility. The subject is not merely visible to an observer; alternatives have been made visible to him, and his reckoning can be tested by whether it crosses the costly route.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight supplies the capacity through which a disclosed route becomes actionable knowledge.
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B002: Supposition supplies the subject's chosen estimate, now tested against disclosed alternatives rather than hidden facts.
  - 90:10 **وَهَدَيْنَٰهُ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a path or truth supplies directional disclosure rather than compulsion.
  - 90:10 **ٱلنَّجْدَيْنِ** ن ج د B002: Emergence and becoming manifest make the two elevated alternatives visually or cognitively exposed.
  - 90:11 **ٱقْتَحَمَ** ق ح م B002: Perilous incursions and hard roads supply the costly movement that bare awareness has not yet achieved.
  - 90:11 **ٱلْعَقَبَةَ** ع ق ب B012: The steep difficult rise gives the disclosed choice material resistance and a visible threshold.
  - 90:12 **أَدْرَىٰكَ** د ر ي B001: Knowledge from the non-dominant mapped root makes explicit the cognitive passage from seeing a route to understanding its demand.

## free the watched neck
- reading: The gaze turns outward: the live test is whether he notices the isolated, constrained person and converts attention into release.
- mechanism: The later 'neck' carries both the direct bodily/legal branch and a branch of watching and waiting. Joined to release from enclosure, it reverses the focus gaze: the man worried about whether anyone sees him is confronted with a constrained person whom someone must notice and free.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing supplies the attention whose direction can turn from the actor's exposure toward another person's constraint.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B005: Individual isolation supplies the social condition in which a bound person can remain unattended.
  - 90:13 **فَكُّ** ف ك ك B002: Releasing a pledge or neck from closure and captivity supplies the concrete intervention demanded of the observer.
  - 90:13 **رَقَبَةٍ** ر ق ب B004: The neck branch keeps the activation anchored in the explicit object of release.
  - 90:13 **رَقَبَةٍ** ر ق ب B001: Watching and waiting cross-activate the neck as a person held under a gaze or awaiting attentive action.

## boast audit recipient trace
- reading: The criterion becomes recipient-side and material: did the claimed pile become nourishment precisely where hunger made the transfer costly and visible?
- mechanism: The later feeding scene supplies an outcome metric for the earlier wealth claim. Piled wealth is not made morally visible by the speaker's declared depletion; it becomes traceable when it crosses into another person's nourishment during severe hunger.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B001: Accounting supplies the comparison between claimed outflow and an observable recipient-side result.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing supplies outcome visibility rather than dependence on the spender's self-report.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B001: Spoken utterance supplies the self-authored account whose sufficiency the later outcome tests.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Accumulated wealth supplies the resource stock available for conversion into relief.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: Piled mass supplies the boast's quantity and heightens the demand for a commensurate trace.
  - 90:14 **إِطْعَٰمٌ** ط ع م B002: Feeding another supplies the concrete transfer by which wealth becomes visible outside the claimant.
  - 90:14 **مَسْغَبَةٍ** س غ ب B001: Hunger joined with exhaustion and famine supplies the urgent recipient condition against which spending acquires weight.

## no one repopulated one by one
- reading: The later singulars turn the question around: how can he posit 'no one' while failing to recognize one nearby isolated person after another?
- mechanism: The empty class 'no one' is later repopulated by indefinite singular persons: an isolated orphan and an abased poor person, one of them explicitly near. The visual question reverses direction, asking not only who sees the powerful actor but which one-by-one persons he has rendered socially unseen.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye or insight supplies recognition, allowing the visual relation to reverse toward neglected persons.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B002: Exhaustive no one supplies the apparently empty human set that the later singular figures refill.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B005: Isolation individual by individual bridges the focus term to the later socially isolated singulars.
  - 90:15 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B002: A thing cut off and without its peer supplies singular social isolation as a concrete human condition.
  - 90:15 **مَقْرَبَةٍ** ق ر ب B001: Nearness defeats any spatial excuse for failing to notice the isolated person.
  - 90:16 **مِسْكِينًا** س ك ن B006: The abasement of destitution supplies a socially lowered person liable to disappear from the powerful actor's field.
  - 90:16 **مَتْرَبَةٍ** ت ر ب B002: Poverty figured as clinging to dust gives that social invisibility a material, ground-level location.

## mutual counsel as caring witness
- reading: A coexisting reading makes visibility communal and reparative: people keep one another seen through trusted counsel, endurance, mercy, and company.
- mechanism: Trust, reciprocal counsel, sustained commitment, mercy, and preserving companionship form a distributed witness network. This coexists with punitive surveillance but changes its social texture: being seen can mean being held in truthful, patient, merciful relation rather than merely being caught.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B004: Mutual visibility supplies the reciprocal relation that the later plural practices socialize.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B005: Isolation supplies the condition overcome by reciprocal counsel and companionship.
  - 90:17 **ءَامَنُوا۟** ء م ن B001: Settled security and trust keep communal observation from reducing to hostile scrutiny.
  - 90:17 **وَتَوَاصَوْا۟ وَتَوَاصَوْا۟** و ص ي B003: Exchanging counsel among a group supplies a many-to-many accountability circuit.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلصَّبْرِ** ص ب ر B003: Bearing a commitment and remaining with it gives that circuit temporal endurance.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلْمَرْحَمَةِ** ر ح م B001: Tender mercy supplies the affective mode in which truthful mutual attention can operate.
  - 90:18 **أَصْحَٰبُ** ص ح ب B002: Preservation through companionship turns witnessing into sustained protective presence.

## covered signs become closed enclosure
- reading: The context distinguishes invisibility from covering: he suppresses manifest signs, only for the desired blind spot to return as a closed and still conspicuous enclosure.
- mechanism: The closing context distinguishes lack of sight from active covering. Manifest signs are covered, while a fire perceptible from afar ends inside an emphatically closed enclosure. The subject's imagined private blind spot thus hardens into chosen occlusion and then reverses into an enclosure that marks and contains him.
- trace:
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B002: Supposition supplies the initial imagined invisibility that the closing reversal materializes.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing supplies the contested relation between manifest evidence, its coverer, and the final visible consequence.
  - 90:19 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B001: Covering and concealing supply an active operation that replaces mere absence of perception.
  - 90:19 **بِـَٔايَٰتِنَا** ء ي ي B003: A manifest sign supplies visible evidence available to be recognized or deliberately covered.
  - 90:20 **نَارٌ** ن و ر B003: A fire perceived from afar defeats the fantasy that enclosure necessarily removes the subject from visibility.
  - 90:20 **مُّؤْصَدَةٌۢ** و ص د B001: Closing tightly over something supplies the final material reversal of the self-chosen blind spot.

## self narration without counter report
- reading: He supposes his self-narration cannot be met by a counter-report because no observed event can travel through another narrator.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B003: Relaying reports and poetry recasts an eyewitness event as evidence that can be narrated onward.
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B010: Seeking out and testing information supplies the inquiry by which a self-authored report might be checked.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B007: A saying that spreads among people supplies the social channel through which the boast can circulate or be countered.

## wealth as cognitive cushion
- reading: Piled wealth props and cushions that miscalculation, making material insulation feel like insulation from sight and account.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B008: A small cushion or prop materializes the support on which the subject's confidence rests.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Accumulated wealth supplies the social and material padding that can make the subject feel insulated.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B001: Material layered one part over another gives the imagined insulation thickness and weight.

## human image held in an eye
- reading: He imagines his human form leaves no image in another eye, although the context makes the human and the eye mutually image-bearing.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B006: Mirror, visible appearance, and facial sign supply the human as an image held on a perceiving surface.
  - 90:4 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B005: The 'human of the eye' and human image in its black center make personhood itself a visual imprint.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B001: The literal observing eye gives the image-bearing surface an explicit bodily location.


# Focus 90:8

## made pair of sight
- reading: The question points to an intentionally produced, beneficiary-directed pair of seeing organs.
- mechanism: Making or bringing into being is directed toward the bodily organ of sight, while the dual fixes the endowment as a coordinated pair rather than an abstract faculty.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B001: Bringing something into being supplies the deliberate production of the endowment.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B001: The bodily seeing eye identifies the made pair as organs of sight.

## capacity for direct presence
- reading: The two eyes are an installed capacity to stand in direct, eyewitness relation to what confronts the person.
- mechanism: The making is read as equipping the person with a standing condition of eyewitness access: the person is not merely alive but made able to meet what is present face to face.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B002: Turning something into a state supplies the installation of an enduring perceptual condition.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B002: Seeing face to face turns the eyes from anatomy alone into access to manifest encounter.

## pair of watchful care
- reading: The eyes also stage a relation of care and custody, ambiguously received by and required from their beneficiary.
- mechanism: The pair can carry a custodial reading in two directions at once: signs of care under which the person was made, and a capacity entrusted to the person for attentive care.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B001: Deliberate making keeps the custodial capacity grounded as a created endowment.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B003: The eye of watchful care supplies protection, supervision, and attentive regard as live functions of the pair.

## two forward sentinels
- reading: The two eyes actively reconnoiter the world ahead of the person's movement and choice.
- mechanism: The body is configured as a small protected domain whose two eyes go ahead of action, detecting approach, exposure, route, and risk.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B002: Making something assume a state supplies the body's equipment as a guarded, reconnaissance-capable condition.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B005: The seeing scout makes each eye a forward sentinel serving the embodied person.

## twin visible sources
- reading: The eyes can also be twin outward sources through which an inner response becomes visible.
- mechanism: Without replacing the bodily sense, the paired eyes become two potential sources from which an inward condition can emerge outward, most concretely through tears.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B001: Bringing into being supplies the establishment of two embodied sources.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B006: The springing water-eye makes flow and visible emergence latent within the bodily pair.

## calibrated for hardship
- reading: The eyes are deliberately proportioned equipment for finding a way through an arduous human condition.
- mechanism: The earlier creation language adds measurement and design, while hardship supplies the operating environment; the eyes read as calibrated equipment for strenuous embodied life.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B001: Deliberate making supplies the focus endowment that the creation cues recalibrate.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B001: The seeing organs are the concrete equipment placed into the strenuous setting.
  - 90:4 **خَلَقْنَا** خ ل ق B001: Measuring a thing to its proportion contributes calibration rather than accidental anatomy.
  - 90:4 **خَلَقْنَا** خ ل ق B002: Creative origination links the earlier making of the human to the focus making of the eyes.
  - 90:4 **كَبَدٍ** ك ب د B002: Struggle and endurance provide the difficult environment in which perceptual equipment must operate.

## human appearing in the eye
- reading: The person is also an image capable of appearing within another eye, so the gift of sight carries mutual exposure rather than one-way mastery.
- mechanism: The context's branch naming the human image in the pupil reverses visual direction: the human made with eyes is also a human who appears inside an eye and can be seen by another.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B001: Making into being anchors the inversion in the created human body.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B002: Face-to-face seeing supplies the encounter in which seer and seen exchange positions.
  - 90:4 **ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ** ء ن س B005: The human image in the eye's black pupil places the human literally within the visual organ's lexical image.

## self carried witness
- reading: The eyes are self-carried witnesses that make imagined invisibility morally unstable.
- mechanism: Repeated supposition culminates immediately before the focus in the claim that no one saw; the made eyes then become evidence carried by the claimant that visibility and accountable perception are already built into human life.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B001: The deliberate making makes perceptual accountability constitutive rather than accidental.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B002: Eyewitness presence converts the anatomical pair into a capacity for manifest evidence.
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B002: Supposition supplies the mistaken mental model that the focus question interrupts.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: Seeing by eye and insight directly activates the focus organs as witnesses.
  - 90:7 **أَحَدٌ** ء ح د B002: The branch of exhaustive negation sharpens the person's fantasy that absolutely no observer exists.

## perception to testimony circuit
- reading: The eyes are the intake side of an accountable communicative circuit whose output can become testimony, claim, or counsel.
- mechanism: A prior claim of speech and the immediate continuation with tongue and lips place the eyes inside an input-output circuit: perception receives evidence, while articulated speech releases an answer into the social world.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B001: The seeing organ supplies the perceptual input side of the circuit.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B001: Voiced saying supplies the claimant's output that can be tested against what is seen.
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B001: The tongue as organ of speech supplies the articulating instrument paired with visual intake.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B002: Speech passing from lip to lip makes the circuit interpersonal rather than private.

## dual wayfinding
- reading: The paired eyes disclose traversable alternatives and make the person a route-reading agent before choice.
- mechanism: The paired scouts of the focus are followed by guidance toward two elevated and manifest routes; sight becomes a wayfinding capacity that exposes alternatives before movement.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B005: The seeing scout supplies reconnaissance ahead of the chooser's movement.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B002: Direct visual encounter makes the routes manifest rather than merely reported.
  - 90:10 **وَهَدَيْنَٰهُ** ه د ي B001: Gentle indication toward a road or truth assigns navigational work to the perceptual pair.
  - 90:10 **ٱلنَّجْدَيْنِ** ن ج د B001: Elevation gives the two routes a terrain profile that scouts can survey.
  - 90:10 **ٱلنَّجْدَيْنِ** ن ج د B002: Appearance and clarity make route-disclosure the result of guidance and sight.

## seeing does not cross
- reading: Eyes can survey and size the hard route, but their very adequacy exposes the separate failure to understand and cross it.
- mechanism: An eye-estimation branch inside the verb of plunging meets the difficult raised obstacle and the question of knowledge: vision can size the terrain and knowledge can identify it, but neither performs the crossing.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B001: The bodily eye supplies the faculty that estimates the visible challenge.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B005: The scout image makes assessment precede entry into dangerous terrain.
  - 90:11 **ٱقْتَحَمَ** ق ح م B007: The eye's estimating the measure of a thing directly links visual appraisal to the withheld plunge.
  - 90:11 **ٱلْعَقَبَةَ** ع ق ب B012: The hard raised obstacle supplies the terrain that sight can inspect but agency must cross.
  - 90:12 **أَدْرَىٰكَ** د ر ي B001: Knowledge distinguishes understanding what the obstacle is from merely viewing its surface.

## watchfulness reoriented to release
- reading: Watchful eyes are a morally directional power: they can police bondage or become attentive guardians of release.
- mechanism: The watchful and sentinel potentials of the eyes meet a phrase in which the watched or guarded neck is to be freed; visual vigilance becomes ethically ambivalent and must be redirected from policing captivity toward protecting release.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B003: Watchful care supplies the protective use of sustained attention.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B005: The sentinel supplies the potentially coercive or liberating practice of surveillance.
  - 90:13 **فَكُّ** ف ك ك B002: Freeing a pledged or captive neck assigns watchfulness the goal of release rather than confinement.
  - 90:13 **رَقَبَةٍ** ر ق ب B001: Monitoring and waiting expose the surveillance potential latent in the neck-root.
  - 90:13 **رَقَبَةٍ** ر ق ب B002: Guarding supplies the live alternative between guarding a captive and guarding a person's freedom.

## sight that detects claims of need
- reading: The eyes also locate another person's bodily and relational difficulty, converting visibility into an actionable claim.
- mechanism: Direct and scouting sight is given concrete social targets: exhausting hunger, a child cut off from a caregiver yet near in kinship, and a person abased by poverty against the dust. Seeing changes from intake into diagnosis of another's actionable claim.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B002: Face-to-face seeing supplies direct encounter with need rather than distant abstraction.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B005: The scout function turns the eyes into detectors of where material and relational deficits lie.
  - 90:14 **إِطْعَٰمٌ** ط ع م B002: Feeding another supplies the action demanded once bodily lack has been perceived.
  - 90:14 **مَسْغَبَةٍ** س غ ب B001: Hunger joined with exhaustion gives sight a concrete physiological emergency to recognize.
  - 90:15 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: A child cut off from a caregiver supplies a visible relational absence, not only material lack.
  - 90:15 **مَقْرَبَةٍ** ق ر ب B003: Kinship makes the observed need close enough to carry a particular relational claim.
  - 90:16 **مِسْكِينًا** س ك ن B006: The abasement of destitution supplies the social condition that attentive eyes must not overlook.
  - 90:16 **مَتْرَبَةٍ** ت ر ب B002: Poverty figured as clinging to dust gives deprivation a low, bodily, spatial visibility.

## two views make reciprocal depth
- reading: The pair models relational knowledge: depth emerges when distinct views are linked, just as counsel becomes truthful through patient and merciful reciprocity.
- mechanism: Two sightlines can yield depth rather than duplication; the context's linking and reciprocal-counsel branches transpose that binocular relation into a community where perspectives are joined and disciplined by patience and mercy.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B001: The literal pair of seeing organs supplies two coordinated sightlines.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B002: Direct encounter supplies the shared reality toward which multiple viewpoints are oriented.
  - 90:17 **وَتَوَاصَوْا۟ وَتَوَاصَوْا۟** و ص ي B001: Linking one thing to another supplies the integration that turns plurality into depth.
  - 90:17 **وَتَوَاصَوْا۟ وَتَوَاصَوْا۟** و ص ي B003: Exchanging counsel among a group makes the integrated viewpoints reciprocal rather than centralized.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلصَّبْرِ** ص ب ر B001: Restraint against panic supplies the temporal discipline needed to hold another view in relation to one's own.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلْمَرْحَمَةِ** ر ح م B001: Tender mercy supplies the affective orientation governing how joined viewpoints are used.

## bilateral orientation
- reading: The pair also embodies the capacity to orient within a field that can divide into opposed sides and lasting associations.
- mechanism: The bilateral eye-pair is reactivated by mirrored companion groups assigned to right and left sides; embodied laterality becomes an orientation device for opposed communal outcomes.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B001: The literal two eyes supply a bodily left-right architecture.
  - 90:18 **أَصْحَٰبُ** ص ح ب B001: Companionship makes the right-side outcome a persisting social location rather than a solitary label.
  - 90:19 **أَصْحَٰبُ** ص ح ب B001: Repeated companionship mirrors the first group on the opposed side.
  - 90:18 **ٱلْمَيْمَنَةِ** ي م ن B002: The right hand and rightward side supply one pole of the embodied orientation.
  - 90:19 **ٱلْمَشْـَٔمَةِ** ش ء م B001: The left side supplies the opposed pole completing the lateral field.

## visible signs covered and sealed
- reading: The eyes guarantee only access: visible signs can still be actively covered, and refused openness can terminate in enclosure.
- mechanism: The made eyes open onto manifest signs, but covering blocks recognition and tight closure converts the final luminous fire into an enclosing medium; failure of sight becomes active refusal rather than lack of equipment.
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B002: Direct seeing supplies access to what is manifest and therefore the possibility of recognition.
  - 90:19 **بِـَٔايَٰتِنَا** ء ي ي B003: The manifest sign supplies the visible content toward which the endowed eyes are answerable.
  - 90:19 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B001: Covering supplies an active obstruction laid over what sight could disclose.
  - 90:19 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B003: Veiling truth specifies that the perceptual failure concerns recognition, not optical absence.
  - 90:20 **نَارٌ** ن و ر B002: Kindled fire makes the final visible medium painful and inescapably present.
  - 90:20 **مُّؤْصَدَةٌۢ** و ص د B001: Tightly shutting a door supplies the terminal enclosure that prevents any outward opening.

## two present assets
- reading: The two eyes briefly resonate as valuable assets already settled in the person's possession, relativizing the boast about dissipated heaps.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:8 **نَجْعَل** ج ع ل B005: A stipulated reward or consideration makes the act of making resonate with assigned value.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B011: Present tangible cash turns the two eyes into an image of wealth already in hand.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: Acquiring and amassing wealth supplies the economic register against which bodily assets are revalued.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: Abundance piled layer upon layer supplies the claimed mass of wealth contrasted with two immediately present assets.

## twin springs of response
- reading: The paired eyes can become springs through which seeing suffering flows outward as tears, mercy, and movement toward relief.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B006: The springing water-eye supplies two visible sources from which response can flow.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: The non-dominant quenching branch adds water and relief as a cross-domain activation without replacing the surface verb of seeing.
  - 90:14 **مَسْغَبَةٍ** س غ ب B001: Hunger with exhaustion supplies the suffering that can draw a fluid, affective response from sight.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلْمَرْحَمَةِ** ر ح م B001: Tender mercy gives the outward flow an ethical orientation toward relief.

## apertures against the seal
- reading: The eyes become gifted apertures onto relation and release, set against the closing image of a world sealed over its inhabitants.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B007: The leaking eye-hole supplies a thin opening through which an inside can meet an outside.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B009: The eye-like socket or receptacle gives the bodily pair a material aperture geometry.
  - 90:13 **فَكُّ** ف ك ك B001: Opening what is locked supplies the ethical expansion of aperture into release.
  - 90:20 **مُّؤْصَدَةٌۢ** و ص د B001: Closing and sealing over something supplies the terminal negation of aperture.
  - 90:20 **مُّؤْصَدَةٌۢ** و ص د B001: Tight door closure sharpens the final state as an enclosure with no usable opening.

## gaze as moral force
- reading: A gaze can act upon another person, so the endowed eyes carry responsibility for whether attention wounds, polices, protects, or has mercy.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B004: Striking or affecting with the eye supplies an active, potentially harmful force of gaze.
  - 90:13 **رَقَبَةٍ** ر ق ب B001: Monitoring supplies the social extension of gaze into sustained power over another.
  - 90:13 **فَكُّ** ف ك ك B002: Release from captivity supplies a constructive direction in which visual power can be reoriented.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلْمَرْحَمَةِ** ر ح م B001: Tender mercy supplies the affective alternative to a wounding or objectifying gaze.


# Focus 90:9

## articulation apparatus
- reading: A deliberately coordinated speech apparatus whose asymmetry—one mobile tongue within two lips—makes articulation possible.
- mechanism: A mobile inner articulator works against a paired outer boundary, converting breath and bodily motion into differentiated speech.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B001: The organ-of-speech image supplies the mobile articulator at the mechanism's center.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B001: The lip and labial-letter image supplies the paired outer articulators and their shaping boundary.

## relational relay
- reading: A relational channel by which a message crosses from one embodied person to another.
- mechanism: The tongue forms transmissible language and messages, while the lips place that content into face-to-face circulation between mouths.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B006: The language, report, and message scope makes the tongue a carrier of content that can outlive its first utterance.
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B008: The conveyed-message image gives the tongue a relay function rather than mere sound production.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B002: The mouth-to-mouth address image makes the relay interpersonal, embodied, and reciprocal-capable.

## social force and reputation
- reading: A socially consequential instrument that can confront, persuade, and leave a reputation circulating among people.
- mechanism: Articulate speech confronts, argues, and then circulates as public mention; the endowment therefore carries social force and reputational consequences.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B002: The verbal taking or confrontation image gives the tongue force directed at another person.
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B003: The eloquence and argumentative-power image supplies speech's ability to persuade or overpower.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B004: The good-public-mention image supplies the reputational residue left among other people.

## request resource pressure
- reading: The mouth as a pressure point where need is voiced and finite social resources are claimed.
- mechanism: The mouth is not only an outlet for expression; it is where need becomes an audible claim upon finite stores and upon another person's attention.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B001: The speech-organ image supplies the means by which a need can be voiced.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B003: The repeated-asking image connects the lips to demand that can occupy or exhaust a finite resource.

## performative bind and release
- reading: Tongue and lips form a performative threshold through which voiced commitments can help bind or release social states.
- mechanism: Oath images bind speech to parties, release images undo obligation or captivity, and the tongue-and-lips channel stands between those states as a performative threshold: voiced language can participate in closing or opening a social bond.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B006: The word, report, and message scope supplies linguistic content capable of carrying a public commitment.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B002: The face-to-face oral-address image supplies the embodied event in which a commitment can be heard by another.
  - 90:1 **أُقْسِمُ** ق س م B004: The oath apportioned among its parties supplies binding public speech and a relation among those addressed.
  - 90:2 **حِلٌّۢ** ح ل ل B005: The release or discharge of an oath supplies the inverse operation: a spoken bond can be loosened.
  - 90:13 **فَكُّ** ف ك ك B002: The freeing of a pledge or captive enlarges verbal unbinding into embodied social release.
  - 90:13 **رَقَبَةٍ** ر ق ب B004: The neck or person image fixes the release mechanism on an embodied subject rather than an abstraction.
  - 90:18 **ٱلْمَيْمَنَةِ** ي م ن B003: The oath sense reactivates binding speech late in the sequence and keeps the legal-performative reading live.

## speech as generated artifact
- reading: The mouth generates consequential verbal artifacts, including the possibility of manufactured speech, that remain attributable to their producer.
- mechanism: Generation, fabrication, and strenuous emergence recast utterance as something the mouth brings forth: words are produced artifacts for which their producer remains responsible, not weightless emissions.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B006: The word and message scope supplies the product brought forth by the mouth.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B001: The paired lips supply the bodily boundary across which the produced utterance emerges.
  - 90:3 **وَوَالِدٍ وَلَدَ** و ل د B005: The image of one thing arising from another supplies a general production relation beyond literal descent.
  - 90:4 **خَلَقْنَا** خ ل ق B007: The fabrication-of-speech image makes verbal production capable of manufacture and distortion.
  - 90:4 **كَبَدٍ** ك ب د B002: The hardship and exertion image supplies the labor through which an embodied product comes forth.

## claim accountability circuit
- reading: The mouth is the auditable output channel through which concealed calculations become claims exposed to observation and answerability.
- mechanism: Repeated private supposition becomes an audible first-person claim, while seeing and the paired eyes establish observation; the tongue and lips therefore sit in a perception-to-claim circuit in which inner calculation becomes inspectable speech.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B006: The report and spoken-expression scope supplies the outward claim that can be evaluated.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B002: The oral-address image makes the claim publicly embodied rather than merely inward.
  - 90:5 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B002: The supposition image supplies the first hidden calculation that speech will expose.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B001: The voiced-saying image converts the private calculation into an explicit claim.
  - 90:7 **أَيَحْسَبُ** ح س ب B002: The repeated supposition image shows that the mistaken inner model persists after the boast.
  - 90:7 **يَرَهُۥٓ** ر ء ي B001: The eye-and-insight image supplies the observation against which the claim is accountable.
  - 90:8 **عَيْنَيْنِ** ع ي ن B003: The watchful-care image turns the paired eyes immediately before the focus into an oversight function.

## dual route articulation
- reading: The paired lips form a micro-topography in which the tongue routes speech, making articulation itself an image of directed choice.
- mechanism: A tongue-like extension moving within two lips is spatialized by guidance toward two elevations; articulation becomes micro-navigation, with an utterance taking shape by passing through paired boundaries and selecting a direction.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B004: The slender tongue-shaped extension image supplies a moving projection that can be imagined as tracing a route.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B001: The paired lips supply the two visible boundaries through which the route of articulation passes.
  - 90:10 **وَهَدَيْنَٰهُ** ه د ي B001: The gentle indication toward a path supplies directional guidance for the articulatory movement.
  - 90:10 **وَهَدَيْنَٰهُ** ه د ي B009: The non-dominant split's arduous-descent image keeps a hazardous counter-route live beside gentle guidance.
  - 90:10 **ٱلنَّجْدَيْنِ** ن ج د B001: The elevation image gives the paired destinations topographic resistance and moral effort.

## distributive mouth
- reading: The mouth is a distributive threshold where a voiced claim can move sustenance from accumulated wealth into an exposed body.
- mechanism: The asking mouth places a claim on wealth piled into a mass; apportionment and feeding move a share across the lips toward a hungry, unsupported, or dirt-bound person. Voice and intake meet at one distributive threshold.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B006: The language and report scope supplies a voice by which need or possession can be stated.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B003: The image of repeated askers exhausting a person or resource directly makes the lips a site of distributive pressure.
  - 90:1 **أُقْسِمُ** ق س م B003: The apportioned-share image makes stored resources divisible and capable of directed transfer.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: The accumulation-of-wealth image supplies the store upon which need makes a claim.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: The mass piled layer upon layer makes accumulation spatially contrast with an apportioned share.
  - 90:14 **إِطْعَٰمٌ** ط ع م B002: The feeding-another image supplies the transfer of sustenance across the recipient's mouth.
  - 90:14 **مَسْغَبَةٍ** س غ ب B001: The hunger-with-exhaustion image supplies urgent bodily demand rather than abstract poverty.
  - 90:15 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: The child cut off from a caregiver supplies a need whose ordinary provisioning channel has failed.
  - 90:16 **مَتْرَبَةٍ** ت ر ب B002: The poverty-as-clinging-to-dirt image places the recipient at the material floor of the distribution system.

## reciprocal counsel network
- reading: Tongue and lips are nodes in a reciprocal ethical network whose repeated traffic in restraint and mercy produces durable companionship.
- mechanism: Messages pass mouth-to-mouth in both directions, repeatedly carrying restraint and mercy; this reciprocal traffic stabilizes trust and turns separate speakers into companions.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B008: The conveyed-message image supplies content intentionally relayed from one person to another.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B002: The mouth-to-mouth address image supplies an embodied interpersonal edge in the network.
  - 90:17 **ءَامَنُوا۟** ء م ن B001: The settled trust image supplies the receptive state in which counsel can circulate without social rupture.
  - 90:17 **وَتَوَاصَوْا۟ وَتَوَاصَوْا۟** و ص ي B003: The exchange-of-counsel image supplies reciprocal rather than one-way transmission and is repeated across both contents.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلصَّبْرِ** ص ب ر B001: The self-restraint image supplies one regulating payload carried by the exchange.
  - 90:17 **بِٱلْمَرْحَمَةِ** ر ح م B001: The tenderness and mercy image supplies the second regulating payload, softening restraint into care.
  - 90:18 **أَصْحَٰبُ** ص ح ب B001: The companionship-and-continuance image supplies the durable social relation produced by repeated exchange.

## lips reveal or seal
- reading: Lips are an ethical gate: the boundary enabling disclosure can also cover signs, suppress language, and become a seal.
- mechanism: The lips become a gate over language: they can open so visible signs are voiced and illuminated, or close so truth is covered and what burns remains trapped inside an enclosure. The same boundary that enables articulation can reverse into sealing.
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B006: The language and report scope supplies content that can either be disclosed or withheld.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B001: The paired physical lips supply a boundary capable of opening for articulation or closing over the tongue.
  - 90:19 **كَفَرُوا۟** ك ف ر B003: The truth-obscuring image supplies epistemic covering as the negative operation of the gate.
  - 90:19 **بِـَٔايَٰتِنَا** ء ي ي B003: The manifest-sign image supplies what is available to be recognized and voiced rather than covered.
  - 90:20 **نَارٌ** ن و ر B001: The illumination image supplies disclosure's positive possibility.
  - 90:20 **نَارٌ** ن و ر B002: The burning-fire image supplies the destructive state that remains when energy is enclosed rather than illuminating.
  - 90:20 **مُّؤْصَدَةٌۢ** و ص د B001: The dominant mapped closure image scales the closed lips into a total enclosure.
  - 90:20 **مُّؤْصَدَةٌۢ** و ص د B001: The non-dominant mapped door-sealing image sharpens the mouth-boundary analogy into a gate closed fast.

## split speech as minimization
- reading: The tongue also frames quantity, making accumulated wealth rhetorically smaller even while boasting of its mass.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B003: The argumentative-eloquence image supplies a speaker's capacity to frame quantities persuasively.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B002: The oral-address image places that framing into an audible boast before others.
  - 90:6 **يَقُولُ** ق و ل B001: The non-dominant split's smallness image introduces minimization inside the act of saying.
  - 90:6 **مَالًا** م و ل B001: The wealth-accumulation image supplies the quantity whose presentation can be rhetorically managed.
  - 90:6 **لُّبَدًا** ل ب د B002: The piled-mass image intensifies the tension between actual abundance and verbal small-making.

## mouth as nourishment release trigger
- reading: The mouth becomes a trigger within a nourishment economy, exposing how provision may depend on—and must not merely exploit—a vulnerable intermediary.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B007: The pastoral induction image supplies a vulnerable young intermediary whose presence releases nourishment and is then withdrawn.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B003: The repeated-asking image connects the mouth to pressure on food and water supplies.
  - 90:14 **إِطْعَٰمٌ** ط ع م B002: The feeding-another image converts released nourishment into an ethical transfer.
  - 90:14 **مَسْغَبَةٍ** س غ ب B001: The exhausted-hunger image supplies the bodily need that makes nourishment release urgent.
  - 90:15 **يَتِيمًا** ي ت م B001: The child severed from a caregiver activates concern for a young dependent used or left outside normal provision.

## speech as social cordage
- reading: Repeated mouth-to-mouth counsel twists separate verbal strands into the durable cordage of companionship.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 90:9 **وَلِسَانًا** ل س ن B009: The fiber-preparation image supplies separate strands made ready to be twisted into a stronger line.
  - 90:9 **وَشَفَتَيْنِ** ش ف ه B002: The mouth-to-mouth image supplies contact points along which verbal strands pass.
